# Separate terminal and common-square vectors

MAIN-AUTHORED UNVERIFIED algebraic advisory for a FUTURE coefficient law.
It does not open a third mechanism in any active worker allocation.
Original target OPEN; no program or formal call.

Use the original conjugate-coordinate notation of the fully read
7911357c6aefc16c: y=e*x0-T*g, terminal center -s*x0+v^T y,
e^T v=p+s, alpha_star=p, v=k-alpha, p>s>0. Let
eta=(p-s)/(p+s), K*e=0, K symmetric PSD, and choose an INDEPENDENT
common-square column z with e^T z=p+s. Let e^T zeta=0 and set

 P=K+v*v^T+eta*z*z^T,
 A^T=-A, A*e=d=zeta+(p-s)(v-z),
 N=T^T(P+A).

Use the same exact original signed matching:
lambda_(n,star)=2p,lambda_(n,i)=2alpha_i,
lambda_(i,j)=-N_ij for distinct prefix indices,
mu=N*e,nu=N^T*e-2alpha, with no incoming terminal rows.
Value flow cancels exactly as in the original row algebra. Direct expansion
gives the COMPLETE original-space identity

 M(q)=||g_n+s*x0-v^T y||^2+y^T K y
       +eta||z^T y-(p+s)x0||^2
       +(q-p^2)||x0||^2+<x0,zeta^T y>.               (1)

Indeed Pe=(p+s)v+(p-s)z, so
2s*v-Pe+d=-2(p-s)z+zeta, and
eta(p+s)^2=p^2-s^2. These are precisely the mixed and initial terms
needed to complete the common square. Setting z=v recovers the previous
rank-one alignment, which is therefore a restriction of this architecture.

The outgoing optimum vector remains

 mu=T^T(Pe+d)=2p*R+T^T zeta, R=T^T v.

The incoming optimum vector and its two invariants become

 nu=K*t+(t^T v)*v+eta*(t^T z)*z-A*t-2alpha,
 e^T nu=2p*(t^T v)+zeta^T t-2(1-p),
 t^T nu=t^T Kt+(t^T v)^2+eta*(t^T z)^2-2alpha^T t.

For weighted variance K=diag(w)-w*w^T/sum(w), completing the actual
zero-sum zeta term pays J=(1/4)sum zeta_i^2/w_i. Thus(1) is PSD iff
q>=p^2+J, by the same explicit common/detail square-zero argument when
every w_i>0. Crucially, the PAID imbalance is zeta, while A*e=d can also
contain the zero-sum difference(p-s)(v-z). This is not a free arbitrary
skew correction: every resulting finite and optimum row still needs its
sign proved, and the common vector z must be explicitly selected.

No nonnegative matching has been constructed here. In particular zeta=0
would make any hypothetical legal matching prove the generally impossible
q=p^2; the Huber lower witness prevents interpreting positivity of(1)
alone as success. A future law must pay enough global charge and prove ALL
row signs. The prior absolute-tail metric/star-imbalance moment obstruction,
if proved using only the nonnegative clock-square terms, may survive this
extension. No existing exclusion is claimed escaped merely by adding z.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to determine whether lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or rigorously disprove it by a full-function-class asymptotic separation. Here W_n(h) is the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||²) over every finite-dimensional convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start, under the actual predetermined nonnegative word h. W_n^*=inf_(h>=0)W_n(h), W=2R, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n is the exact all-integer-split benchmark. The goal is unchanged.

The signed components are the exact benchmark envelope and conditional separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`), finite-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and actual H3 U law (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`), and the single-cut Huber portfolio with its precise insufficiency (`b98bb807d514e80d`). None contains the original-target proof or disproof.

The current constructive architecture uses one finite projection body, reverse atom splits preserving a complete future trajectory, and an original-radius estimate. Its best new operation repairs a support condition by moving only walls that were unsaturated at every relevant later query. The operation is an unverified exact draft; its available monotone-face implementation has a proved-in-draft whole-word ceiling below the benchmark. A coupled repeated-face implementation is not established as viable.

The main missing statement is quantitative global attainment with the original radius. The alternative upper route still lacks an actual correlated-row payment for its left-query normal fan and vector terms. These are method failure results; there is no proposition failure result for the original limit-one claim. PARTIAL does not mean nearly solved: each original-target route still has an unmatched theorem-strength interface.

| Item | Current mathematical state |
|---|---|
| Fixed goal | UNCHANGED: lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a conclusive asymptotic full-class disproof |
| Verified complete proof | NO |
| Verified counterexample to the original claim | NO |
| Signed-closed supporting components | Three: exact benchmark comparison; finite-word projection transfer/H3; single-cut portfolio and its scope |
| Main missing statement | A uniform all-word original-radius lower witness, or an all-depth full-class upper certificate |
| False or obsolete methods | Exact local-variance greedy inversion; specified two-point PP look-ahead; monotone active-face reserves at selected0; fixedlambda>1 full retention; independent single-cut banks |
| Highest-priority unresolved task | P0: construct and bound a genuinely coupled repeated-face trajectory, or pay the actual full upper inequality; another parameterization is insufficient |

| Sub-task | Status | Closed facts | Conditional or draft material | Main missing interface |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Exact benchmark and normalization | CLOSED | `931b9653ce0f7201` | None needed for its stated scope | None for the benchmark comparison itself |
| Finite-word full-class projection transfer | CLOSED | `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` | No all-depth inference | A U inequality for every relevant body/start at the final depth |
| Low-total-mass lower bound | CLOSED | `b98bb807d514e80d` gives W>=1/(1+2sum h) | None for sum h<=(B_n-1)/2 | The complementary high-mass words |
| Uniform high-mass lower bound | PARTIAL | No closing fact | Exact inverse operations and scoped failed constructions | Legal global coupling and original variance at the exact benchmark |
| All-depth separating upper bound | PARTIAL | `931b9653ce0f7201`, `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` only at their stated scopes | Joint leaf reserve and actual right-normal payment | Preserve a sufficient inequality on all inherited children |

Current best proof skeleton:

1. Keep the exact capacities C0=B0=1, C_n=max Omega(C_k,C_l), B_n=max G(C_k,B_l) over all k+l=n-1, where Omega=(x+y+sqrt(x²+6xy+y²))/2 and G=2x+y/2+sqrt(y²+8xy)/2.
2. The supplied Zhang--Jiang upper schedule gives W_n^*<=1/B_n.
3. The signed Huber bound covers every word with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, including zero steps (`b98bb807d514e80d`).
4. On other words, a finite-law construction must specify one fixed function, all original physical iterates, its original selected minimizer and its full original radius.
5. Existing inverse operations preserve chronology but do not give the needed uniform cost. Their complete monotone implementation loses a fixed benchmark factor; an independent reflected bank returns to the old single-cut portfolio.
6. A proved uniform cost-to-terminal-value bound, after all admissibility and asymptotic constants are checked, would supply the remaining lower inequality and close the affirmative target.
7. Independently, a full-class bound W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for all sufficiently large inherited depths would close the negative direction using `931b9653ce0f7201`; `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies a legitimate projection-body route to that premise, not the premise itself.

Central missing lemma: there must exist a deterministic sequence eta_n>=0 tending to0, independent of the chosen word, such that for every sufficiently large n and every h in[0,infinity)^n with sum h>(B_n-1)/2, one can specify a finite-dimensional convex1-smooth f, an original minimizer x_star, and x0!=x_star whose actual GD endpoint satisfies 2(f(x_n)-f_star)>= (1-eta_n)||x0-x_star||²/B_n. No such construction is established. Stating this interface is not new progress: its uniform attainment is the original missing minimax strength, and any proposed recursive substitute must actually prove it rather than rename it.

## 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:

- C_n<=(n+1)^rho and B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho, rho=log_2(1+sqrt2); the actual inherited hierarchy has B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50 — `931b9653ce0f7201`.
- Equality of the finite-word full supremum with an origin-vertex projection-simplex supremum, preserving original selected minimizers and radii; actual same-parameter H3 U law for all trailingd>=1 — `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`.
- W_n(h)>=max{1/(1+2sumh),max_i (h_i-1)_+²/[(1+prefix_i)²(1+2suffix_i)]}, and this portfolio alone is asymptotically insufficient — `b98bb807d514e80d`.

| Route | Status | Concrete reason and exact scope |

| Two-point primitive-form look-ahead using benchmark or prefix-sum capacities | FALSE AS STATED | Draft exact minimizer remains Huber at every inverse step and has benchmark-normalized payoff<(94/97)² |

## 2. Interface contract table

### Exact benchmark to the low-mass region

Input required: a word h>=0 of lengthn with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, using the exact all-splitB_n. Output claimed: W_n(h)>=1/B_n. Failure mode if ignored: a formerly saturated coordinate changes its recorded future when its wall moves. Status: PARTIAL.

Input required: an actual full trajectory from the preceding interface and an original selected minimizer valid for the FINALbody, plus a bound on its original squared radius relative to terminal2F. Output claimed: a uniform payoff>=(1-o(1))/B_n. Available fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies benchmark comparisons only. ### Bridge 3 — preserve a sufficient all-body upper inequality

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, every compact convexK containing0, everyx, and everyd>=1, prove U_d(T_Hm x)<=U_(d+(32/17)S_m)(x) on the literal same-parameterH_m; a weaker statement restricted tod=1 is sufficient if established directly for all bodies/starts. ## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to determine whether lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or rigorously disprove it by a full-function-class asymptotic separation. Here W_n(h) is the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||²) over every finite-dimensional convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start, under the actual predetermined nonnegative word h. W_n^*=inf_(h>=0)W_n(h), W=2R, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n is the exact all-integer-split benchmark. The goal is unchanged.

The signed components are the exact benchmark envelope and conditional separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`), finite-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and actual H3 U law (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`), and the single-cut Huber portfolio with its precise insufficiency (`b98bb807d514e80d`). None contains the original-target proof or disproof.

The current constructive architecture uses one finite projection body, reverse atom splits preserving a complete future trajectory, and an original-radius estimate. Its best new operation repairs a support condition by moving only walls that were unsaturated at every relevant later query. The operation is an unverified exact draft; its available monotone-face implementation has a proved-in-draft whole-word ceiling below the benchmark. A coupled repeated-face implementation is not established as viable.

The main missing statement is quantitative global attainment with the original radius. A new upper derivation keeps both true input projections p0=P(x/M) and ell=P(x/A). The normal rows inside the actual parent remainder cancel the previous left-query fan at the SAME retained fraction. A root normal with negative coefficient is transported through the exact identity Np+Nr=<k p+D r,p-r>. 2. The supplied Zhang--Jiang upper schedule gives W_n^*<=1/B_n.
3. The signed Huber bound covers every word with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, including zero steps (`b98bb807d514e80d`).
4. On other words, a finite-law construction must specify one fixed function, all original physical iterates, its original selected minimizer and its full original radius.
5. Existing inverse operations preserve chronology but do not give the needed uniform cost. Their complete monotone implementation loses a fixed benchmark factor; an independent reflected bank returns to the old single-cut portfolio.
6. A proved uniform cost-to-terminal-value bound, after all admissibility and asymptotic constants are checked, would supply the remaining lower inequality and close the affirmative target.
7. Independently, a full-class bound W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for all sufficiently large inherited depths would close the negative direction using `931b9653ce0f7201`; `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies a legitimate projection-body route to that premise, not the premise itself.

Central missing lemma: there must exist a deterministic sequence eta_n>=0 tending to0, independent of the chosen word, such that for every sufficiently large n and every h in[0,infinity)^n with sum h>(B_n-1)/2, one can specify a finite-dimensional convex1-smooth f, an original minimizer x_star, and x0!=x_star whose actual GD endpoint satisfies 2(f(x_n)-f_star)>= (1-eta_n)||x0-x_star||²/B_n. No such construction is established. Stating this interface is not new progress: its uniform attainment is the original missing minimax strength, and any proposed recursive substitute must actually prove it rather than rename it.

## 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:

- C_n<=(n+1)^rho and B_n<=(12/5)(n+1)^rho, rho=log_2(1+sqrt2); the actual inherited hierarchy has B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50 — `931b9653ce0f7201`.
- Equality of the finite-word full supremum with an origin-vertex projection-simplex supremum, preserving original selected minimizers and radii; actual same-parameter H3 U law for all trailingd>=1 — `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`.
- W_n(h)>=max{1/(1+2sumh),max_i (h_i-1)_+²/[(1+prefix_i)²(1+2suffix_i)]}, and this portfolio alone is asymptotically insufficient — `b98bb807d514e80d`.

Failed or obsolete routes:

| Route | Status | Concrete reason and exact scope |
|---|---|---|
| Exact current-variance greedy inverse from benchmark-scaled +/-Q | FALSE AS STATED | Draft all-depth cap bootstrap gives Var0>Q*(7/5)^(2^(m-3)-1) on completeH_m; global prefix optimization is not excluded |
| Two-point primitive-form look-ahead using benchmark or prefix-sum capacities | FALSE AS STATED | Draft exact minimizer remains Huber at every inverse step and has benchmark-normalized payoff<(94/97)² |
| Movable-wall reserves that freeze once backward | FALSE AS STATED | Draft covers every forward nested-active-set K_a trajectory at original selected0: payoff<=1/D_m; repeated reentry and other minima with zero walls are outside scope |
| Independent paid-reflection reserve bank | OBSOLETE | Its component payoff is exactly the signed single-cut term `b98bb807d514e80d`; positive independent sums give weighted averages |
| Direct termwise two-remainder induction with eta1=3/4 | FALSE AS STATED | Draft left-fan allocation requires1-eta'>=(1-eta)/theta withtheta<3/4 at relevant joins; that concrete retained fraction fails |
| Every fixedlambda>1 fully retained displacement mixture | FALSE AS STATED | Draft actual triangle violates E>=Rem_lambda for sufficiently large trailingd; its explicit fullUgap remains positive |
| Discard both actual child surpluses after true-input transport | FALSE AS STATED | Actual interval data at arbitrarily deep inherited internal levels give a strict negative remainder for every common eta<=1,gamma>=0 and fixed discounted kappa<1; the invariant is not refuted |
| Below-apex alternating-two-edge translated triangle | FALSE AS STATED | As a benchmark witness policy, its entire allowed geometric class has payoff<=33/(32D_m), hence normalized payoff<1551/1600; other face patterns remain open |
| Positive conic hull of variable-step interval A/D modes | FALSE AS STATED | Combined physical-weight cuts and nonnegative diagonal slack still require central r/(s+1)<=sqrt2-1, violated by actual H_m before any terminal penalty |

These draft exclusions are not signed facts and are not whole-function-class counterexamples. The final arguments and original-radius scopes are recorded in R10_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md, R10B_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md and R11_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md with their exact source proofs.

## 2. Interface contract table

### Exact benchmark to the low-mass region

Input required: a word h>=0 of lengthn with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, using the exact all-splitB_n. Output claimed: W_n(h)>=1/B_n. Available fact: `b98bb807d514e80d` gives the actual Huber witness1/(1+2sumh). Missing verification on the actual model: none for this region. Failure mode if ignored: a proof restricted to this region omits every high-total-mass word. Status: CLOSED.

### Inverse atom geometry to a legal full trajectory

Input required: finite positive probability weights, E X=0, fixed nonnegative walls a with Ea>0, K_a={g:Eg0,g>=-a}; at each inverse h=1+d>1, Z=t+da-Y>=0 and E[omega Z]=dEa. A wall change must preserve every previously used physical and auxiliary projection. Output claimed: actual predecessors X_L=Y-ha and X_R=t-a+Z/d, one final fixed function, and an original squared radius E X0² at selected0. Available facts: none certifies this new operation; the detailed manual identity and chronological construction are draft evidence. Missing verification: (1) preserve the necessary unsaturated coordinates through repeated reentries, or pay every altered old sample; (2) cover the remaining physical step ranges in any all-word rule. Failure mode if ignored: a formerly saturated coordinate changes its recorded future when its wall moves. Status: PARTIAL.

### Legal trajectory to an exact-profile lower bound

Input required: an actual full trajectory from the preceding interface and an original selected minimizer valid for the FINALbody, plus a bound on its original squared radius relative to terminal2F. Output claimed: a uniform payoff>=(1-o(1))/B_n. Available fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies benchmark comparisons only. Missing verification: (1) a globally coupled cost estimate escaping the completeH_m obstructions; (2) a uniform all-word selection including zeros and very large steps; (3) other-minimum accounting if walls vanish. Failure mode if ignored: rare reflected atoms can make the original variance enormous, and a later minimum/radius can conceal that cost. Status: UNKNOWN.

### Projection-body inequality to a full-class asymptotic disproof

Input required: for all sufficiently largem, the literal inheritedH_m and every compact convexK containing0, every startx, and at least trailingd=1 satisfy U_1(T_Hm x)<=U_(1+(32/17)S_m)(x), whereU_d(x)=max_(g in K)(2<x,g>-d||g||²). Output claimed: W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), and hence liminf W_n^*/U_n^ZJ<=799/800<1. Available facts: finite-word transfer `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`, benchmark comparison `931b9653ce0f7201`. Missing verification: the entire all-depth inequality; H3 and the new draft leaf reserve do not supply it. Failure mode if ignored: replacing a child by a primitive or fully retained block assumes the unproved property. Status: PARTIAL.

## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

“The primitive joining root appears, so the inverse children compose.” Status: DANGEROUS. Root matching determines scalar coefficients only. The actual support guard, all earlier auxiliary samples, and the full original prefix cost remain real conditions; the specified two-point policy demonstrably stays below the benchmark.

“More reserve atoms make the wall repair available indefinitely.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the monotone-freezing invariant at selected0. The draft constructs that invariant exactly and decomposes its entire nested-face trajectory into charged one-release modes and an affine mode. All remain below the required benchmark on completeH_m.

“An independent collection of reflected reserves is a new coupled adversary.” Status: OBSOLETE. Its component is the exact single-cut term in `b98bb807d514e80d`; an orthogonal positive sum produces a radius-weighted average. It creates no new cross-coordinate gain.

“A bound normalized at0 bounds every selected minimizer when walls vanish.” Status: DANGEROUS. Zero walls can create a polar minimum cone. A different original minimum must be valid for the final fixed body, and its original distance must be proved; the selected0 ceiling does not automatically transfer.

“Paying the input and right transport normals closes the upper induction.” Status: DANGEROUS. The draft now pays those normals using actual samples. Yet the exact remainder is negative on legal interval data if both child surpluses are discarded. The actual invariant is positive there, so this is failure of the payment rule, not the proposition.

“Positive interval modes provide a new way to retune the diagonal certificate.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the displayed dictionary. The central-cut obstruction extends to every finite positive sum of A/D atoms, with arbitrary positive within-interval weights and nonnegative physical diagonal slack. Non-diagonal remainders or different function weights are outside that result, but need a real certificate.

“A positive Brownian-bridge covariance proves the dominance residual positive.” Status: DANGEROUS. Only one summand is automatically PSD. The exact residual also includes signed diagonal and interpolation terms; its entire matrix must be proved PSD with actual chosen coefficients.

Concrete anti-routes:

- Do not revive the current-variance greedy inverse by changing only tie-breaking: the whole inherited cap bootstrap is the obstruction.
- Do not call the two-point PP policy a global prefix optimizer: its cost is exact on the chosen flat trajectory, not on every possible intermediate law.
- Do not replace coupled repeated-face motion by independent interval components: their exact payoff is already the single-cut portfolio.
- Do not increase a formerly strictly saturated wall while retaining its old gradient record: that changes the actual function queries.
- Do not reset the inherited hierarchy's parameters or the original radius at a child.
- Do not promote a conditional benchmark implication or any scoped draft ceiling into the original asymptotic answer.
- Do not discard actual child surplus after substituting common eta/gamma bounds: the completed payment fails on a literal inherited trajectory.
- Do not allocate positive interval-weight retuning under the same physical-weight/diagonal-slack assumptions: the whole positive cone is excluded.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — a genuinely coupled complete-hierarchy witness

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, with eps1/(4m),beta1-eps,c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps),a_h=beta/c and the SAME-parameter recursionH_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_hc^j,H_j), construct one finite-dimensional convex1-smooth function with an attained ORIGINALminimum and a full literalH_m trajectory having payoff>=1/B_(2^m-1), or explicitly demonstrate why the proposed coupled construction cannot do so. Prerequisites: all walls/weights/geometry fixed before execution; every original iterate and denominator retained; actual repeated face exits/reentries if using K_a at selected0. Existing fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` gives the strict benchmark ratio that makes1/D_m insufficient. Missing checks: real recycling correlations and their original variance; the current monotone and independent constructions do not provide them. Closure criterion: an actual all-depth witness with a full quantitative payoff, not an abstract state recurrence. This is independent of the upper bridge and is only a lower-route viability test, not the original all-word conclusion.

### Bridge 2 — uniform high-mass all-word attainment

Target statement: the central missing lemma in Section0, with eta_n->0 independent of h, for every h>=0 withsumh>(B_n-1)/2. Prerequisites: a legal global construction whose terminal value and original radius are estimated, with original step order and all-integerB_n retained. Existing fact: `b98bb807d514e80d` closes only the complementary low-mass region. Missing checks: (1) a selection rule for arbitrary words rather than only H_m or a chosen concatenation tree; (2) all nonnegative step ranges; (3) a constant-preserving asymptotic estimate. Closure criterion: every quantifier in the original uniform lower bound follows. This bridge is dependent on a genuinely useful construction and remains theorem-strength; naming it supplies no reduction in the work.

### Bridge 3 — preserve a sufficient all-body upper inequality

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, every compact convexK containing0, everyx, and everyd>=1, prove U_d(T_Hm x)<=U_(d+(32/17)S_m)(x) on the literal same-parameterH_m; a weaker statement restricted tod=1 is sufficient if established directly for all bodies/starts. Prerequisites: actual child projection rows, justified convex mixtures, and an induction statement that genuinely holds on the children. Existing facts: H3 at the stronger undiscouted mass (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`) and the separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`). Missing checks: a quantitative transport of the actual child surplus or a genuinely different complete inequality; the remaining expression after discarding both surpluses is false on actual inherited data. Closure criterion: a full original-space inequality for every required depth, followed by the signed transfer and benchmark comparison. This bridge is independent of the lower construction; its truth is not assumed.


### Bridge 4 — instantiate a full non-diagonal dominance certificate

Target statement: for every sufficiently large inherited m, choose explicit weights1>rho_0>...>rho_n>0, u_i=rho_i h_i fori<n, and u_n=1+(32/17)S_m-sum_(i<n)u_i>0. Letell_i=h_i fori<n andell_n=u_n/rho_n, an algebraic weight only. Choose a zero-row-sum matrixC with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sumsb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i. With the ACTUAL trajectory matrixBmat, prove the FULL matrix

    R=K+diag(u_i(1-ell_i)+b_i)-(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T)

is PSD, whereK_ij=ell_iell_j[min(rho_i,rho_j)-rho_i rho_j]. The exact covariance identity and doubled dominance expansion are in R11_BROWNIAN_BRIDGE_DOMINANCE_LEAD_20261009.md. Prerequisites: the same physical word, original interpolation rows and original selected minimum; no extra terminal update, no assumption of independent gradients, and no replacement of full PSD by a convenient set of diagonal signs. Existing signed fact:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies the benchmark implication after a full upper bound. The covariance and matrix identities are only draft evidence, not signed facts. Missing checks: actualrho law, actual nonnegativeC, full positivity uniformly in inheritedm, and a novelty comparison with the exact old source-weight families. Closure criterion: nonnegativity of the complete original-radius dominance residual for every actual smooth-convex trajectory, yieldingW(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m]. This is independent of the geometric lower construction and is presently only a sufficient candidate class; naming its matrix problem does not solve it.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to determine whether lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or rigorously disprove it by a full-function-class asymptotic separation. Here W_n(h) is the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||²) over every finite-dimensional convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start, under the actual predetermined nonnegative word h. W_n^*=inf_(h>=0)W_n(h), W=2R, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n is the exact all-integer-split benchmark. The goal is unchanged.

The signed components are the exact benchmark envelope and conditional separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`), finite-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and actual H3 U law (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`), and the single-cut Huber portfolio with its precise insufficiency (`b98bb807d514e80d`). None contains the original-target proof or disproof.

The lower construction has now realized genuinely coupled repeated faces on one translated triangle, but its complete below-apex, alternating-two-edge, nonbinding-third-face class still misses the benchmark. The new concrete certificate candidates fixrho_i=1-T_i/(2S) orrho_i=(S+1)/(S+1+T_i), u_i=rho_i h_i before the terminal query, andu_n=1+(32/17)S-sum_(i<n)u_i. These are specified whole-word functionals, not proved inequalities. The boundaryrho_0=1 is retained explicitly. The upper architecture must retain quantitative actual child surplus or directly build a complete original-radius dominance certificate. A new draft covariance identity retains a non-diagonal Brownian-bridge remainder and changes the function-value weights; no actual weights or complete PSD certificate have been found. Neither architecture is established as viable.

The main missing statement remains a quantitative original-radius lower witness or an all-depth full-class upper inequality. The two true input projections do cancel the old normal fan, but an actual arbitrarily deep interval trajectory makes the entire remaining expression negative if both child surpluses are discarded. The child and parent invariant themselves remain positive on that example. Keeping signed energies gives an exact recurrence with an unpaid signed right-child term; it is not a closed certificate. These are method failure results; there is no proposition failure result for the original limit-one claim. PARTIAL does not mean nearly solved: each original-target route still has an unmatched theorem-strength interface.

- Do not increase a formerly strictly saturated wall while retaining its old gradient record: that changes the actual function queries.
- Do not reset the inherited hierarchy's parameters or the original radius at a child.
- Do not promote a conditional benchmark implication or any scoped draft ceiling into the original asymptotic answer.
- Do not discard actual child surplus after substituting common eta/gamma bounds: the completed payment fails on a literal inherited trajectory.
- Do not allocate positive interval-weight retuning under the same physical-weight/diagonal-slack assumptions: the whole positive cone is excluded.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — a genuinely coupled complete-hierarchy witness

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, with eps1/(4m),beta1-eps,c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps),a_h=beta/c and the SAME-parameter recursionH_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_hc^j,H_j), construct one finite-dimensional convex1-smooth function with an attained ORIGINALminimum and a full literalH_m trajectory having payoff>=1/B_(2^m-1), or explicitly demonstrate why the proposed coupled construction cannot do so. Prerequisites: all walls/weights/geometry fixed before execution; every original iterate and denominator retained; actual repeated face exits/reentries if using K_a at selected0. Existing fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` gives the strict benchmark ratio that makes1/D_m insufficient. Missing checks: real recycling correlations and their original variance; the current monotone and independent constructions do not provide them. Closure criterion: an actual all-depth witness with a full quantitative payoff, not an abstract state recurrence. This is independent of the upper bridge and is only a lower-route viability test, not the original all-word conclusion.

### Bridge 2 — uniform high-mass all-word attainment

Target statement: the central missing lemma in Section0, with eta_n->0 independent of h, for every h>=0 withsumh>(B_n-1)/2. Prerequisites: a legal global construction whose terminal value and original radius are estimated, with original step order and all-integerB_n retained. Existing fact: `b98bb807d514e80d` closes only the complementary low-mass region. Missing checks: (1) a selection rule for arbitrary words rather than only H_m or a chosen concatenation tree; (2) all nonnegative step ranges; (3) a constant-preserving asymptotic estimate. Closure criterion: every quantifier in the original uniform lower bound follows. This bridge is dependent on a genuinely useful construction and remains theorem-strength; naming it supplies no reduction in the work.

### Bridge 3 — preserve a sufficient all-body upper inequality

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, every compact convexK containing0, everyx, and everyd>=1, prove U_d(T_Hm x)<=U_(d+(32/17)S_m)(x) on the literal same-parameterH_m; a weaker statement restricted tod=1 is sufficient if established directly for all bodies/starts. Prerequisites: actual child projection rows, justified convex mixtures, and an induction statement that genuinely holds on the children. Existing facts: H3 at the stronger undiscouted mass (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`) and the separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`). Missing checks: a quantitative transport of the actual child surplus or a genuinely different complete inequality; the remaining expression after discarding both surpluses is false on actual inherited data. Closure criterion: a full original-space inequality for every required depth, followed by the signed transfer and benchmark comparison. This bridge is independent of the lower construction; its truth is not assumed.


### Bridge 4 — instantiate a full non-diagonal dominance certificate

Target statement: for every sufficiently large inherited m, instantiate one of the fixed profiles in R12_FIXED_DOMINANCE_PROFILES_20261009.md (or an explicitly derived replacement), with1=rho_0>...>rho_n>0 and u_i=rho_i h_i fori<n, and u_n=1+(32/17)S_m-sum_(i<n)u_i>0. Letell_i=h_i fori<n andell_n=u_n/rho_n, an algebraic weight only. Choose a zero-row-sum matrixC with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sumsb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i. With the ACTUAL trajectory matrixBmat, prove the FULL matrix

    R=K+diag(u_i(1-ell_i)+b_i)-(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T)

is PSD, whereK_ij=ell_iell_j[min(rho_i,rho_j)-rho_i rho_j]. The exact covariance identity and doubled dominance expansion are in R11_BROWNIAN_BRIDGE_DOMINANCE_LEAD_20261009.md. Prerequisites: the same physical word, original interpolation rows and original selected minimum; no extra terminal update, no assumption of independent gradients, and no replacement of full PSD by a convenient set of diagonal signs. Existing signed fact:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies the benchmark implication after a full upper bound. The covariance and matrix identities are only draft evidence, not signed facts. Missing checks: actualrho law, actual nonnegativeC, full positivity uniformly in inheritedm, and a novelty comparison with the exact old source-weight families. Closure criterion: nonnegativity of the complete original-radius dominance residual for every actual smooth-convex trajectory, yieldingW(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m]. This is independent of the geometric lower construction and is presently only a sufficient candidate class; naming its matrix problem does not solve it.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to determine whether lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or rigorously disprove it by a full-function-class asymptotic separation. Here W_n(h) is the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||²) over every finite-dimensional convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start, under the actual predetermined nonnegative word h. W_n^*=inf_(h>=0)W_n(h), W=2R, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n is the exact all-integer-split benchmark. The goal is unchanged.

The signed components are the exact benchmark envelope and conditional separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`), finite-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and actual H3 U law (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`), and the single-cut Huber portfolio with its precise insufficiency (`b98bb807d514e80d`). None contains the original-target proof or disproof.

The lower construction has now realized genuinely coupled repeated faces on one translated triangle, but its complete below-apex, alternating-two-edge, nonbinding-third-face class still misses the benchmark. Both initial fixed profiles, rho_i=1-T_i/(2S) andrho_i=(S+1)/(S+1+T_i), are now refuted by a static Huber trajectory of every sufficiently large ODD completeH_m, at the original radius. The resulting D_u<0 excludes FULLPSD for EVERY admissibleC, not merely interval modes. The only new fixed candidate here is ProfileC: rho_i=1-T_i/(8S), u_i=rho_i h_i before the terminal query, andu_n=1+(32/17)S-sum_(i<n)u_i. It passes the known critical-orbit calibration but has no complete certificate. The boundaryrho_0=1 is retained explicitly. The upper architecture must retain quantitative actual child surplus or directly build a complete original-radius dominance certificate. A new draft covariance identity retains a non-diagonal Brownian-bridge remainder and changes the function-value weights; no actual weights or complete PSD certificate have been found. Neither architecture is established as viable.

The main missing statement remains a quantitative original-radius lower witness or an all-depth full-class upper inequality. The two true input projections do cancel the old normal fan, but an actual arbitrarily deep interval trajectory makes the entire remaining expression negative if both child surpluses are discarded. The child and parent invariant themselves remain positive on that example. Keeping signed energies gives an exact recurrence with an unpaid signed right-child term; it is not a closed certificate. These are method failure results; there is no proposition failure result for the original limit-one claim. PARTIAL does not mean nearly solved: each original-target route still has an unmatched theorem-strength interface.

Current best proof skeleton:

1. Keep the exact capacities C0=B0=1, C_n=max Omega(C_k,C_l), B_n=max G(C_k,B_l) over all k+l=n-1, where Omega=(x+y+sqrt(x²+6xy+y²))/2 and G=2x+y/2+sqrt(y²+8xy)/2.
2. The supplied Zhang--Jiang upper schedule gives W_n^*<=1/B_n.
3. The signed Huber bound covers every word with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, including zero steps (`b98bb807d514e80d`).

5. Existing inverse operations preserve chronology but do not give the needed uniform cost. Their complete monotone implementation loses a fixed benchmark factor; an independent reflected bank returns to the old single-cut portfolio.
6. A proved uniform cost-to-terminal-value bound, after all admissibility and asymptotic constants are checked, would supply the remaining lower inequality and close the affirmative target.
7. Independently, a full-class bound W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for all sufficiently large inherited depths would close the negative direction using `931b9653ce0f7201`; `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies a legitimate projection-body route to that premise, not the premise itself.

Central missing lemma: there must exist a deterministic sequence eta_n>=0 tending to0, independent of the chosen word, such that for every sufficiently large n and every h in[0,infinity)^n with sum h>(B_n-1)/2, one can specify a finite-dimensional convex1-smooth f, an original minimizer x_star, and x0!=x_star whose actual GD endpoint satisfies 2(f(x_n)-f_star)>= (1-eta_n)||x0-x_star||²/B_n. No such construction is established. Stating this interface is not new progress: its uniform attainment is the original missing minimax strength, and any proposed recursive substitute must actually prove it rather than rename it.

## 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

These draft exclusions are not signed facts and are not whole-function-class counterexamples. The final arguments and original-radius scopes are recorded in R10_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md, R10B_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md and R11_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md with their exact source proofs.

The actual support guard, all earlier auxiliary samples, and the full original prefix cost remain real conditions; the specified two-point policy demonstrably stays below the benchmark.

Current best proof skeleton:

1. Keep the exact capacities C0=B0=1, C_n=max Omega(C_k,C_l), B_n=max G(C_k,B_l) over all k+l=n-1, where Omega=(x+y+sqrt(x²+6xy+y²))/2 and G=2x+y/2+sqrt(y²+8xy)/2.
2. The supplied Zhang--Jiang upper schedule gives W_n^*<=1/B_n.
3. The signed Huber bound covers every word with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, including zero steps (`b98bb807d514e80d`).
4. On other words, a finite-law construction must specify one fixed function, all original physical iterates, its original selected minimizer and its full original radius.
5. Existing inverse operations preserve chronology but do not give the needed uniform cost. Their complete monotone implementation loses a fixed benchmark factor; an independent reflected bank returns to the old single-cut portfolio.
6. A proved uniform cost-to-terminal-value bound, after all admissibility and asymptotic constants are checked, would supply the remaining lower inequality and close the affirmative target.
7. Independently, a full-class bound W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for all sufficiently large inherited depths would close the negative direction using `931b9653ce0f7201`; `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies a legitimate projection-body route to that premise, not the premise itself.

Central missing lemma: there must exist a deterministic sequence eta_n>=0 tending to0, independent of the chosen word, such that for every sufficiently large n and every h in[0,infinity)^n with sum h>(B_n-1)/2, one can specify a finite-dimensional convex1-smooth f, an original minimizer x_star, and x0!=x_star whose actual GD endpoint satisfies 2(f(x_n)-f_star)>= (1-eta_n)||x0-x_star||²/B_n. No such construction is established. Stating this interface is not new progress: its uniform attainment is the original missing minimax strength, and any proposed recursive substitute must actually prove it rather than rename it.

## 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:

- W_n(h)>=max{1/(1+2sumh),max_i (h_i-1)_+²/[(1+prefix_i)²(1+2suffix_i)]}, and this portfolio alone is asymptotically insufficient — `b98bb807d514e80d`.

These draft exclusions are not signed facts and are not whole-function-class counterexamples. The final arguments and original-radius scopes are recorded in R10_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md, R10B_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md, R11_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md and R12_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md with their exact source proofs.

## 2. Interface contract table

### Exact benchmark to the low-mass region

Input required: a word h>=0 of lengthn with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, using the exact all-splitB_n. Output claimed: W_n(h)>=1/B_n. Available fact: `b98bb807d514e80d` gives the actual Huber witness1/(1+2sumh). Missing verification on the actual model: none for this region. Failure mode if ignored: a proof restricted to this region omits every high-total-mass word. Status: CLOSED.

### Inverse atom geometry to a legal full trajectory

Input required: finite positive probability weights, E X=0, fixed nonnegative walls a with Ea>0, K_a={g:Eg0,g>=-a}; at each inverse h=1+d>1, Z=t+da-Y>=0 and E[omega Z]=dEa. A wall change must preserve every previously used physical and auxiliary projection. Output claimed: actual predecessors X_L=Y-ha and X_R=t-a+Z/d, one final fixed function, and an original squared radius E X0² at selected0. Available facts: none certifies this new operation; the detailed manual identity and chronological construction are draft evidence. Missing verification: (1) preserve the necessary unsaturated coordinates through repeated reentries, or pay every altered old sample; (2) cover the remaining physical step ranges in any all-word rule. Failure mode if ignored: a formerly saturated coordinate changes its recorded future when its wall moves. Status: PARTIAL.

### Legal trajectory to an exact-profile lower bound

Input required: an actual full trajectory from the preceding interface and an original selected minimizer valid for the FINALbody, plus a bound on its original squared radius relative to terminal2F. Output claimed: a uniform payoff>=(1-o(1))/B_n. Available fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies benchmark comparisons only. Missing verification: (1) a globally coupled cost estimate escaping the completeH_m obstructions; (2) a uniform all-word selection including zeros and very large steps; (3) other-minimum accounting if walls vanish. Failure mode if ignored: rare reflected atoms can make the original variance enormous, and a later minimum/radius can conceal that cost. Status: UNKNOWN.

### Projection-body inequality to a full-class asymptotic disproof

Input required: for all sufficiently largem, the literal inheritedH_m and every compact convexK containing0, every startx, and at least trailingd=1 satisfy U_1(T_Hm x)<=U_(1+(32/17)S_m)(x), whereU_d(x)=max_(g in K)(2<x,g>-d||g||²). Output claimed: W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), and hence liminf W_n^*/U_n^ZJ<=799/800<1. Available facts: finite-word transfer `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`, benchmark comparison `931b9653ce0f7201`. Missing verification: the entire all-depth inequality; H3 and the new draft leaf reserve do not supply it. Failure mode if ignored: replacing a child by a primitive or fully retained block assumes the unproved property. Status: PARTIAL.

## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

“The primitive joining root appears, so the inverse children compose.” Status: DANGEROUS. Root matching determines scalar coefficients only. The actual support guard, all earlier auxiliary samples, and the full original prefix cost remain real conditions; the specified two-point policy demonstrably stays below the benchmark.

“More reserve atoms make the wall repair available indefinitely.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the monotone-freezing invariant at selected0. The draft constructs that invariant exactly and decomposes its entire nested-face trajectory into charged one-release modes and an affine mode. All remain below the required benchmark on completeH_m.

“An independent collection of reflected reserves is a new coupled adversary.” Status: OBSOLETE. Its component is the exact single-cut term in `b98bb807d514e80d`; an orthogonal positive sum produces a radius-weighted average. It creates no new cross-coordinate gain.

“A bound normalized at0 bounds every selected minimizer when walls vanish.” Status: DANGEROUS. Zero walls can create a polar minimum cone. A different original minimum must be valid for the final fixed body, and its original distance must be proved; the selected0 ceiling does not automatically transfer.

“Paying the input and right transport normals closes the upper induction.” Status: DANGEROUS. The draft now pays those normals using actual samples. Yet the exact remainder is negative on legal interval data if both child surpluses are discarded. The actual invariant is positive there, so this is failure of the payment rule, not the proposition.

“Positive interval modes provide a new way to retune the diagonal certificate.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the displayed dictionary. The central-cut obstruction extends to every finite positive sum of A/D atoms, with arbitrary positive within-interval weights and nonnegative physical diagonal slack. Non-diagonal remainders or different function weights are outside that result, but need a real certificate.

“A positive covariance or Laplacian proves the dominance residual positive.” Status: DANGEROUS. Only one summand is automatically PSD. The exact residual also includes signed diagonal and interpolation terms. Fixed A/B are actually false regardless of the chosen matrix; ProfileC remains open, and the increasing-weight Laplacian candidate is only an exact identity. The entire original-space matrix still needs a proof with actual coefficients.

Concrete anti-routes:

- Do not revive the current-variance greedy inverse by changing only tie-breaking: the whole inherited cap bootstrap is the obstruction.
- Do not call the two-point PP policy a global prefix optimizer: its cost is exact on the chosen flat trajectory, not on every possible intermediate law.
- Do not replace coupled repeated-face motion by independent interval components: their exact payoff is already the single-cut portfolio.
- Do not increase a formerly strictly saturated wall while retaining its old gradient record: that changes the actual function queries.
- Do not reset the inherited hierarchy's parameters or the original radius at a child.
- Do not promote a conditional benchmark implication or any scoped draft ceiling into the original asymptotic answer.
- Do not discard actual child surplus after substituting common eta/gamma bounds: the completed payment fails on a literal inherited trajectory.
- Do not allocate positive interval-weight retuning under the same physical-weight/diagonal-slack assumptions: the whole positive cone is excluded.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — a genuinely coupled complete-hierarchy witness

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, with eps1/(4m),beta1-eps,c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps),a_h=beta/c and the SAME-parameter recursionH_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_hc^j,H_j), construct one finite-dimensional convex1-smooth function with an attained ORIGINALminimum and a full literalH_m trajectory having payoff>=1/B_(2^m-1), or explicitly demonstrate why the proposed coupled construction cannot do so. Prerequisites: all walls/weights/geometry fixed before execution; every original iterate and denominator retained; actual repeated face exits/reentries if using K_a at selected0. Existing fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` gives the strict benchmark ratio that makes1/D_m insufficient. Missing checks: real recycling correlations and their original variance; the current monotone and independent constructions do not provide them. Closure criterion: an actual all-depth witness with a full quantitative payoff, not an abstract state recurrence. This is independent of the upper bridge and is only a lower-route viability test, not the original all-word conclusion.

### Bridge 2 — uniform high-mass all-word attainment

Target statement: the central missing lemma in Section0, with eta_n->0 independent of h, for every h>=0 withsumh>(B_n-1)/2. Prerequisites: a legal global construction whose terminal value and original radius are estimated, with original step order and all-integerB_n retained. Existing fact: `b98bb807d514e80d` closes only the complementary low-mass region. Missing checks: (1) a selection rule for arbitrary words rather than only H_m or a chosen concatenation tree; (2) all nonnegative step ranges; (3) a constant-preserving asymptotic estimate. Closure criterion: every quantifier in the original uniform lower bound follows. This bridge is dependent on a genuinely useful construction and remains theorem-strength; naming it supplies no reduction in the work.

### Bridge 3 — preserve a sufficient all-body upper inequality

Target statement: for every sufficiently largem, every compact convexK containing0, everyx, and everyd>=1, prove U_d(T_Hm x)<=U_(d+(32/17)S_m)(x) on the literal same-parameterH_m; a weaker statement restricted tod=1 is sufficient if established directly for all bodies/starts. Prerequisites: actual child projection rows, justified convex mixtures, and an induction statement that genuinely holds on the children. Existing facts: H3 at the stronger undiscouted mass (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`) and the separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`). Missing checks: a quantitative transport of the actual child surplus or a genuinely different complete inequality; the remaining expression after discarding both surpluses is false on actual inherited data. Closure criterion: a full original-space inequality for every required depth, followed by the signed transfer and benchmark comparison. This bridge is independent of the lower construction; its truth is not assumed.


### Bridge 4 — instantiate a full non-diagonal dominance certificate

Target statement: for every sufficiently large inherited m, instantiate the FIXED ProfileC in R12_PROFILE_C_ADDENDUM_20261009.md, rho_i=1-T_i/(8S), with1=rho_0>...>rho_n>0 and u_i=rho_i h_i fori<n, and u_n=1+(32/17)S_m-sum_(i<n)u_i>0. Letell_i=h_i fori<n andell_n=u_n/rho_n, an algebraic weight only. Choose a zero-row-sum matrixC with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sumsb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i. With the ACTUAL trajectory matrixBmat, prove the FULL matrix

    R=K+diag(u_i(1-ell_i)+b_i)-(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T)

is PSD, whereK_ij=ell_iell_j[min(rho_i,rho_j)-rho_i rho_j]. The exact covariance identity and doubled dominance expansion are in R11_BROWNIAN_BRIDGE_DOMINANCE_LEAD_20261009.md. Prerequisites: the same physical word, original interpolation rows and original selected minimum; no extra terminal update, no assumption of independent gradients, and no replacement of full PSD by a convenient set of diagonal signs. Existing signed fact:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies the benchmark implication after a full upper bound. The covariance and matrix identities are only draft evidence, not signed facts. Missing checks: actualrho law, actual nonnegativeC, full positivity uniformly in inheritedm, and a novelty comparison with the exact old source-weight families. Closure criterion: nonnegativity of the complete original-radius dominance residual for every actual smooth-convex trajectory, yieldingW(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m]. This is independent of the geometric lower construction and is presently only a sufficient candidate class; naming its matrix problem does not solve it.


Within Bridge4, a second concrete candidate is now recorded in R12_EXCESS_WEIGHT_LAPLACIAN_LEAD_20261009.md. KeepBcert=1+(32/17)S andsetu_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i forphysicali, u_n=Bcert-sumphysicalu>=1+(43/68)S>0. Fori<j definew_ij=u_j(u_i-h_i)>=0; its graphLaplacianL hasg^TLg=sumw_ij||g_i-g_j||². With the same actual interpolation conventions andb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=u_n-Bcert, the EXACT remainder isR=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)-(C Bmat+Bmat^TC^T). The original dominance residual equals the nonnegative interpolation sum plusg^TRg. A full all-depthPSDproof would be sufficient for the original negative direction, but neither itsC nor the full sign is known. The canonical Zhang--Jiang physical-weight inequalityu_i>=h_i motivates this direction only on its actual source construction; it is not imported as a theorem aboutH_m. The known critical-Huber calibration passes this candidate at both parities, which does not establish validity. This remains part of the same unresolved full-certificate bridge, independent of the lower-function construction, with no asserted target-level progress from the identity alone.

## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to determine whether lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or rigorously disprove it by a full-function-class asymptotic separation. Here W_n(h) is the supremum of 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||²) over every finite-dimensional convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start, under the actual predetermined nonnegative word h. W_n^*=inf_(h>=0)W_n(h), W=2R, and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n is the exact all-integer-split benchmark. The goal is unchanged.

The signed components are the exact benchmark envelope and conditional separation threshold (`931b9653ce0f7201`), finite-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and actual H3 U law (`1e0bf3f41457d8e4`), and the single-cut Huber portfolio with its precise insufficiency (`b98bb807d514e80d`). None contains the original-target proof or disproof.

The lower construction has now realized genuinely coupled repeated faces on one translated triangle, but its complete below-apex, alternating-two-edge, nonbinding-third-face class still misses the benchmark. Both initial fixed profiles, rho_i=1-T_i/(2S) andrho_i=(S+1)/(S+1+T_i), are now refuted by a static Huber trajectory of every sufficiently large ODD completeH_m, at the original radius. The resulting D_u<0 excludes FULLPSD for EVERY admissibleC, not merely interval modes. ProfileC, rho_i=1-T_i/(8S), remains open after two failed concrete attempts: a fixed unequal-cap saturated recursion is infeasible, and an explicit dense cyclic coefficient matrix has a negative full remainder diagonal. The covariance boundaryrho_0=1 was treated explicitly. All acyclic coefficient flows and, under the stated growing-total/bounded-early-weight hypotheses, every single nonnegative destination law are excluded. A distinct source-grounded candidate uses increasing physical weightsu_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i andthe same prescribed total, producing an exact positive gradient-difference Laplacian inside its full remainder. No complete coefficient array or PSD proof exists for it either. The upper architecture must retain quantitative actual child surplus or directly build a complete original-radius dominance certificate. A new draft covariance identity retains a non-diagonal Brownian-bridge remainder and changes the function-value weights; no actual weights or complete PSD certificate have been found. Neither architecture is established as viable.

The main missing statement remains a quantitative original-radius lower witness or an all-depth full-class upper inequality. A new full-class retained primitive--dominant join is now derived in R12_RELAXED_PD_JOIN_LEAD_20261009.md: withgamma=1+3C/4 andthe prescribed parent/right certificate totals, its two-vector coefficient matrix has a uniform positive margin provided the actual left history coefficient isGamma>=(7/10)C²-C. The genuine leaf inputs are available. The all-depth history input remains UNPROVED and is not routine; the conditional join does not solve the original problem. The two true input projections do cancel the old normal fan, but an actual arbitrarily deep interval trajectory makes the entire remaining expression negative if both child surpluses are discarded. The child and parent invariant themselves remain positive on that example. Keeping signed energies gives an exact recurrence with an unpaid signed right-child term; it is not a closed certificate. These are method failure results; there is no proposition failure result for the original limit-one claim. PARTIAL does not mean nearly solved: each original-target route still has an unmatched theorem-strength interface.

## 2. Interface contract table

### Exact benchmark to the low-mass region

Input required: a word h>=0 of lengthn with sum h<=(B_n-1)/2, using the exact all-splitB_n. Output claimed: W_n(h)>=1/B_n. Available fact: `b98bb807d514e80d` gives the actual Huber witness1/(1+2sumh). Missing verification on the actual model: none for this region. Failure mode if ignored: a proof restricted to this region omits every high-total-mass word. Status: CLOSED.

### Inverse atom geometry to a legal full trajectory

Input required: finite positive probability weights, E X=0, fixed nonnegative walls a with Ea>0, K_a={g:Eg0,g>=-a}; at each inverse h=1+d>1, Z=t+da-Y>=0 and E[omega Z]=dEa. A wall change must preserve every previously used physical and auxiliary projection. Output claimed: actual predecessors X_L=Y-ha and X_R=t-a+Z/d, one final fixed function, and an original squared radius E X0² at selected0. Available facts: none certifies this new operation; the detailed manual identity and chronological construction are draft evidence. Missing verification: (1) preserve the necessary unsaturated coordinates through repeated reentries, or pay every altered old sample; (2) cover the remaining physical step ranges in any all-word rule. Failure mode if ignored: a formerly saturated coordinate changes its recorded future when its wall moves. Status: PARTIAL.

### Legal trajectory to an exact-profile lower bound

Input required: an actual full trajectory from the preceding interface and an original selected minimizer valid for the FINALbody, plus a bound on its original squared radius relative to terminal2F. Output claimed: a uniform payoff>=(1-o(1))/B_n. Available fact: `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies benchmark comparisons only. Missing verification: (1) a globally coupled cost estimate escaping the completeH_m obstructions; (2) a uniform all-word selection including zeros and very large steps; (3) other-minimum accounting if walls vanish. Failure mode if ignored: rare reflected atoms can make the original variance enormous, and a later minimum/radius can conceal that cost. Status: UNKNOWN.

### Projection-body inequality to a full-class asymptotic disproof

Input required: for all sufficiently largem, the literal inheritedH_m and every compact convexK containing0, every startx, and at least trailingd=1 satisfy U_1(T_Hm x)<=U_(1+(32/17)S_m)(x), whereU_d(x)=max_(g in K)(2<x,g>-d||g||²). Output claimed: W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), and hence liminf W_n^*/U_n^ZJ<=799/800<1. Available facts: finite-word transfer `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`, benchmark comparison `931b9653ce0f7201`. ## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

“The primitive joining root appears, so the inverse children compose.” Status: DANGEROUS. Root matching determines scalar coefficients only. The actual support guard, all earlier auxiliary samples, and the full original prefix cost remain real conditions; the specified two-point policy demonstrably stays below the benchmark.

“More reserve atoms make the wall repair available indefinitely.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the monotone-freezing invariant at selected0. The draft constructs that invariant exactly and decomposes its entire nested-face trajectory into charged one-release modes and an affine mode. All remain below the required benchmark on completeH_m.

“An independent collection of reflected reserves is a new coupled adversary.” Status: OBSOLETE. Its component is the exact single-cut term in `b98bb807d514e80d`; an orthogonal positive sum produces a radius-weighted average. It creates no new cross-coordinate gain.

“A bound normalized at0 bounds every selected minimizer when walls vanish.” Status: DANGEROUS. Zero walls can create a polar minimum cone. A different original minimum must be valid for the final fixed body, and its original distance must be proved; the selected0 ceiling does not automatically transfer.

“Paying the input and right transport normals closes the upper induction.” Status: DANGEROUS. The draft now pays those normals using actual samples. Yet the exact remainder is negative on legal interval data if both child surpluses are discarded. The actual invariant is positive there, so this is failure of the payment rule, not the proposition.

“Positive interval modes provide a new way to retune the diagonal certificate.” Status: FALSE AS STATED for the displayed dictionary. The central-cut obstruction extends to every finite positive sum of A/D atoms, with arbitrary positive within-interval weights and nonnegative physical diagonal slack. Non-diagonal remainders or different function weights are outside that result, but need a real certificate.

“A positive covariance or Laplacian proves the dominance residual positive.” Status: DANGEROUS. Only one summand is automatically PSD. The exact residual also includes signed diagonal and interpolation terms. Fixed A/B are actually false regardless of the chosen matrix; ProfileC remains open, and the increasing-weight Laplacian candidate is only an exact identity. The entire original-space matrix still needs a proof with actual coefficients.

Concrete anti-routes:

- Do not revive the current-variance greedy inverse by changing only tie-breaking: the whole inherited cap bootstrap is the obstruction.
- Do not call the two-point PP policy a global prefix optimizer: its cost is exact on the chosen flat trajectory, not on every possible intermediate law.
- Do not replace coupled repeated-face motion by independent interval components: their exact payoff is already the single-cut portfolio.
- Do not increase a formerly strictly saturated wall while retaining its old gradient record: that changes the actual function queries.
- Do not reset the inherited hierarchy's parameters or the original radius at a child.
- Do not promote a conditional benchmark implication or any scoped draft ceiling into the original asymptotic answer.
- Do not discard actual child surplus after substituting common eta/gamma bounds: the completed payment fails on a literal inherited trajectory.
- Do not allocate positive interval-weight retuning under the same physical-weight/diagonal-slack assumptions: the whole positive cone is excluded.

# A prospective quadratic-occupation common column

UNVERIFIED MAIN ANALYTICAL LEAD. No mathematical program or formal call was used. This document does not reassign a live worker or authorize another invocation. It is a possible next mechanism ONLY after the full global-cut report is read and the current invocation is actually terminal. Original minimax Goal OPEN.

Retain the full free-pi notation of the current original-array architecture: Ltri lower triangular, t=Ltri e, h=H_m, n=2^m-1, l=n-1, s=product_(i<n)(h_i-1)>0, q=17/(16D), pi=sqrt(q), E=pi+s, F=pi-s, eta=F/E. Put b=1+a, g=1+beta, and define the unit-curvature PREFIX column

 w_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j), so Ltri w=e and w_n=-s.

The signed occupation weights

 p_i=h_i*w_i/(1+s), i<n,

sum to1 by telescoping w_i-w_(i+1)=h_i*w_i. They are signed, not probabilities. Write

 Tquad=sum_(i<n)h_i*w_i^2,
 Mclock=sum_(i<n)t_i*h_i*w_i,
 p0=b/(1+s).

Define a zero-sum column with zero ORIGINAL source coordinate by

 u=p-p0*e0-(1-p0)*e1.

Then e^T u=0, u_0=0, and the two exact scalar products should be rederived before use:

 w^T u=(Tquad-b^2)/(1+s)+a,
 t^T u=Mclock/(1+s)-(1+b)+b^2/(1+s).

Let r=l-1, the actual penultimate query. Its clock gap to the last query is t_l-t_r=g. Set

 uhat=u-[(t^T u)/g]*(e_l-e_r).

This leaves e^T uhat=0 and uhat_0=0, and makes t^T uhat=0 exactly. Since w_l=s/a and w_r=-s/(a*beta),

 w^T uhat=(Tquad-b^2)/(1+s)+a-[s/(a*beta)]*(t^T u).

Thus imposing the clock condition at the two last original queries changes the quadratic-prefix product only by a factor containing the extremely small full product s. It does NOT introduce a physical step or change any gradient trajectory.

Starting from the old source-optimal column zsrc=(E/a)(b e0-e1), consider the ONE explicit new column

 gamma=q*(1+s)/(2*(q-s^2)),
 znew=zsrc+E*gamma*uhat.

The denominator is positive if s<pi. This candidate preserves EXACTLY

 e^T znew=E,
 t^T znew=-E/a,
 (znew)_0=E*b/a.

Consequently the original Xreq=D-pi^(-2)-eta*(t^T z)^2, source charge calibration, source capacity and source row-clock moment formulas should remain unchanged, provided the complete original polynomial and skew moments are recomputed with this znew. No finite-entry sign conclusion follows from those identities.

The reason for this precise gamma is the proposed global quadratic-prefix cost:

 Tw=(w^2)^T mu+2alpha^T(w^2)-4s*v^T w-4F*z^T w,
 mu=2pi Ltri^T v.

The before-terminal-pool part of (w^2)^T mu contains exactly2q*Tquad. The displayed gamma makes the Tquad contribution of -4F*znew^T w exactly-2q*Tquad, because F*E=q-s^2. This is an algebraic cancellation lead; all tail-pool corrections and all other terms still require a uniform exact bound. The sign of Tw has NOT been established in this advisory.

Remaining obligations before this can be a candidate certificate include:

- Re-derive the full original polynomial, Ae/At compatibility, optimum signs, and source formulas with znew; do not rely on the old sparse common column where it no longer applies.
- Prove explicit bounds on Mclock and on the added last-two coordinates, keeping the SAME enclosing m. The heuristic that exponentially small pi beats polynomial-in-m prefix moments is not a proof. One possible analytic bound uses |w_i|<=beta^i, h_i and t_i bounded by fixed powers of i+1, but every constant/threshold must be supplied.
- Re-evaluate the full squared-clock cost as well as Tw, all earlier terminal-six cuts, and the true endpoint capacities. Large signed common coordinates are not free merely because the x0 square charge stays q.
- Choose an explicit PSD metric and a genuinely nonlocal skew completion, and check every ORIGINAL finite-pair sign. Old local skew fixes are already obstructed. Passing two global costs is not sufficient.

No matrix has been called feasible, no formal submission is authorized here, and no numerical replay or post-budget extraction has been performed.

# R10 parent note: actual inverse cost and future-preserving wall repair

2026-10-09. **UNVERIFIED manual research draft. Original target OPEN.** This is a concrete extension of the finite-law inverse construction, not an all-word minimax theorem. No mathematical program, solver or formal submission was used. It was derived independently by the parent while two bounded R10 native calls were running. Their original allocations are not increased by this note.

## 1. One fixed finite body with variable lower walls

On a finite space with positive weights p_i, let E be the Euclidean space of mean-zero real variables, with inner product E[XY]. Fix a nonnegative variable a with mean a_bar>0 and define

    K_a={g in E: g_i>=-a_i for every i},
    F_a(X)=max_(g in K_a) E[Xg]-E[g^2]/2.

K_a is nonempty, compact and convex; it contains0. F_a is convex1-smooth, has gradient P_(K_a), and attains its minimum0 at the original selected point0. Positive walls give a unique minimum; uniqueness is not needed below. The initial squared radius relative to this selected minimum is E[X0^2], with no replacement by a later radius. A fixed weighted Euclidean isometry identifies this with an ordinary finite-dimensional instance.

The physical projection for a mean-zero X is

    g_i=max(X_i-t,-a_i),   E g=0,

where t>=0. Equivalently E(X-t+a)_+=a_bar. The actual update remains X'=X-hg. The wall labels a, all weights and the final body are fixed before the forward trajectory. A backward atom split duplicates the entire already-built future record, including its current wall label, before an explicitly justified modification.

## 2. Exact inverse formulas, including the wall cross moment

Take h=1+d>1, a mean-zero next law Y, and a real t satisfying

    Z=t+d*a-Y>=0.

Choose a right-branch probability omega_i in[0,1] with E[omega Z]=d*a_bar. The two inverse values are

    X_L=Y-h*a=t-a-Z,
    X_R=t-a+Z/d.

Their projection gradients are -a and -a+Z/d, respectively. Their weighted mean is0, as is their gradient mean, and both physical h updates give the same Y. Thus these are actual inverse points of one projection body. The mean-zero condition also forces t>=0.

Put J=E[omega Z^2], J_a=E[omega a Z], and M_a=E[a^2]. Direct expansion gives

    Var X=Var Y-2h E[aY]+h^2 M_a+2h*t*a_bar
                         -(2h/d)J_a+(d^-2-1)J,
    F_a(X)=t*a_bar+(d+1/2)M_a-E[aY]-(h/d)J_a+J/(2d^2),
    ||P_(K_a)X||^2=M_a-(2/d)J_a+J/d^2.

For the value formula, write X-g=t on the upper branch and X-g=t-Z on the lower branch. Since E g=0, F_a(X)=E[g^2]/2+E[(1-omega)aZ]. This proves the displayed formula without a missing normal contribution.

For nonnegative LOCAL cost coefficients alpha,beta (not the benchmark sequences), define

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)=Var X+2alpha F_a(X)+beta||P_(K_a)X||^2,
    T=h+alpha, C0=1+alpha+beta,
    K0=h^2+alpha(2d+1)+beta.

Then the actual full cost is

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)
     =Var Y-2T E[aY]+K0 M_a+2T*t*a_bar
       -[2(h(1+alpha)+beta)/d]J_a+(C0/d^2-1)J.       (1)

The wall cross moment is real data, not a free scalar reserve.

## 3. The same primitive inverse-cost identity survives

Assume C0>d^2. Define

    w=C0/d^2-1>0, gamma=d+2T/w,
    R=Y+gamma*a.

Choose the unique z with E(R-z)_+=gamma*a_bar. Since E R=gamma*a_bar, one has z>=0. Select all atoms with R>z, optionally a fraction of boundary atoms with R=z, and none with R<z. Let I denote this possibly split selected set, p=P(I)>0, a_I=E[a*1_I]. Set

    t=z+(d+gamma)(a_bar-a_I)/p.                    (2)

This t is nonnegative and gives E[1_I Z]=d*a_bar. It is an admissible inverse threshold if and only if the actual support guard

    t>=max_i(Y_i-d*a_i)                            (3)

holds. Unselected atoms already satisfy (3), since R_i<=z and a_i>=0. Selected atoms still need it. No physical child is rescaled in this construction.

Write U=(R-z)_+. The auxiliary SAME-body projection is

    q=P_(K_a)(Y/gamma)=U/gamma-a.

From w( gamma-d )=2T and direct substitution,

    2(h(1+alpha)+beta)/d=w(d+gamma),
    K0=w*gamma*d.

Therefore the last two terms of (1) combine as w E[1_I Z(t-R)]. Using E U=gamma*a_bar gives

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)
     =Var Y-2T E[aY]+K0 M_a+2T*z*a_bar
                        -w(d+gamma)E[aU]+w E[U^2].             (4)

Also, by expanding the actual q,

    U_gamma(Y):=2gamma F_a(Y/gamma)
       =2z*a_bar+E[U^2]/gamma-2E[aY]-gamma*M_a,
    ||q||^2=E[U^2]/gamma^2-2E[aU]/gamma+M_a.

Consequently (4) is exactly

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)=Var Y+T U_gamma(Y)+B_gamma||q||^2,
    B_gamma=w gamma^2-T gamma=gamma(wd+T)>0.         (5)

Thus the guarded identity from R9 is not limited to equal lower walls. In particular alpha=C_L-1, beta=C_L(C_L-1) and d(C_L+C_R+d)=C_L*C_R yield gamma=C_R and B_gamma=(C_L+d)(C_L+C_R+d), exactly as in the R9 parent note. This remains a conditional attained identity, not a closed composition theorem.

## 4. A genuine future-preserving repair of the support guard

Suppose an entire finite future physical trajectory has already been built with this body, starting at Y and including every future terminal evaluation. Suppose EACH selected atom i in I is unsaturated at every such query: its recorded gradient satisfies

    g_i^(k)=X_i^(k)-t_k

for every future query k. Equality at the clipping boundary is allowed; a strictly clipped lower branch is not.

Increase a_i, only on selected atoms, to any larger finite values. Each old unsaturated projection formula remains unchanged at every future query: max(X_i^(k)-t_k,-a_i) still equals the recorded g_i^(k). Other atoms and all means remain unchanged. Therefore every recorded t_k still solves the projection equation, all future actual gradients/iterates and objective values stay EXACTLY the same. The terminal objective is preserved, and0 remains a legal selected minimizer.

The auxiliary projection q in Section3 is also unchanged. On selected atoms U_i increases by gamma times the wall increase; both E U and gamma*a_bar increase equally. Thus z, selected massp and the set I can be kept fixed (a selected boundary atom may become strictly selected). Because a_bar-a_I is unchanged, (2) keeps the SAME t. On each increased atom Y_i-d*a_i decreases, while unselected atoms already satisfy (3). Choose

    new a_i>=max(old a_i,(Y_i-t)/d)

on the selected atoms. This makes the support guard hold everywhere. Formula (5) then constructs an actual predecessor, at the SAME cost on its right-hand side: VarY, q and U_gamma(Y) did not change.

All choices can be made backwards before the actual forward run. Atom duplication preserves its whole recorded future, and the change above is justified by every recorded query, not only its endpoint. The terminal function is one fixed K_a after all changes; there is no function change during execution.

This is a zero-additional-cost guard repair under an explicit persistent-unsaturation condition. It is NOT permission to increase the wall of an atom that was strictly saturated at any already-built future query. At such a query the old gradient generally changes. Nor does it prove that the selected upper-tail atoms at the next inverse step enjoy the condition.

The hypothesis can be weakened to just the selected atoms that actually require an increase: those with Y_i-d*a_i>t. All other selected walls can be left fixed. Such a violating atom necessarily has Y_i>0, since t>=0 and a_i>=0. This observation narrows the required persistent-unsaturation property; it does not prove it after a reflected inverse branch.

If an already constructed proof also relies on other auxiliary evaluations, their projection records must be included among the samples preserved by this operation, or their changed contributions must be paid separately. Preserving the physical future alone preserves its objective and chronology, but does not automatically preserve a previous auxiliary-potential bookkeeping argument.

## 5. The actual remaining composition requirement

An all-word or full-H_m construction must preserve enough persistent-unsaturated atoms to perform Section4 at every needed inverse join, while controlling the entire prefix cost and terminal scale. The current note does not prove that invariant, nor give an original variance bound at the benchmark scale. It does identify an actual operation that repairs the previous real guard while preserving all built future data; it is stronger than renaming that guard.

Any attempt using this operation must show, for every selected atom and every later physical query, why no strict lower saturation occurred. A high terminal value alone does not imply that history property. If the needed selected atom has already saturated, the free repair is unavailable and the cost of any alternative must be paid explicitly.

No original minimax conclusion, full-class upper bound, formal acceptance, or permission to renew old grants follows. The note is an UNVERIFIED lead for a fresh bounded direct construction, and all original target quantifiers remain unchanged.
